## Names of the data

The index lists 8824 Lean names that the 116 written chapters cite, on a `Lean:` line or in the list of declarations of a section. For each name it gives the file and the line of the declaration, and the chapters that cite it. The lines are those of the readable edition `grh/src` at the commit `72fead102f66`. A link on a line number opens the file at that line on GitHub. This section has the 1443 names in the namespace `New.Data`, the namespace of the admissible data.
